  image: stitched(from chunks[].data.image_data) ZlibStream {
    cinfo: u4be
    cm: u4be enum ZlibMethod {8 = deflate}
    flevel: u2be enum ZlibLevel {0 = fastest, 1 = fast, 2 = default, 3 = best compression}
    fdict: u1be
    fcheck: u5be
    dictid: switch fdict * (3 < remaining) {1 => u32be, _ => bytes[0]}
    compressed: sized(remaining - 4) decoded(deflate) Inflated (contents scanlines) {
      scanlines: sized(remaining) decoded(png scanlines width chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.width
          height chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.height depth chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth
          colour chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.color_type interlace
          chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.interlace) Unfiltered (contents pixels) {
        pixels: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.interlace {
          1 => raster(chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.width by chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.height,
              chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth *
              (chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.color_type == 2 ? 3 :
              (chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.color_type == 4 ? 2 :
              (chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.color_type == 6 ? 4 : 1))) bits a pixel, adam7) switch
              chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.color_type {
            0 => GreyPixel (unit pixel, colour grey) {
              grey: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            2 => RgbPixel (unit pixel, colour red green blue) {
              red: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              green: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              blue: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            3 => IndexedPixel (unit pixel) {
              index: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            4 => GreyAlphaPixel (unit pixel, colour grey alpha alpha) {
              grey: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              alpha: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            6 => RgbaPixel (unit pixel, colour red green blue alpha alpha) {
              red: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              green: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              blue: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              alpha: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            _ => bytes[0]
          }
          _ => raster(chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.width by chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.height,
              chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth *
              (chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.color_type == 2 ? 3 :
              (chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.color_type == 4 ? 2 :
              (chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.color_type == 6 ? 4 : 1))) bits a pixel, rows) switch
              chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.color_type {
            0 => GreyPixel (unit pixel, colour grey) {
              grey: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            2 => RgbPixel (unit pixel, colour red green blue) {
              red: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              green: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              blue: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            3 => IndexedPixel (unit pixel) {
              index: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            4 => GreyAlphaPixel (unit pixel, colour grey alpha alpha) {
              grey: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              alpha: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            6 => RgbaPixel (unit pixel, colour red green blue alpha alpha) {
              red: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              green: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              blue: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              alpha: switch chunks[type = "IHDR"].data.bit_depth {
                1 => u1be
                2 => u2be
                4 => u4be
                8 => u8be
                16 => u16be
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
            }
            _ => bytes[0]
          }
        }
      }
    }
    adler32: u32be  check adler32 over unpacked compressed
  }
}

      50778 = calibration illuminant 1, 50779 = calibration illuminant 2,
      50780 = best quality scale, 50781 = raw data unique id, 50827 = original raw file name,
      50828 = original raw file data, 50829 = active area, 50830 = masked areas,
      50831 = as shot icc profile, 50832 = as shot pre-profile matrix,
      50833 = current icc profile, 50834 = current pre-profile matrix
    }
    type: u16be enum FieldType {
      1 = byte, 2 = ascii, 3 = short, 4 = long, 5 = rational, 6 = signed byte, 7 = undefined,
      8 = signed short, 9 = signed long, 10 = signed rational, 11 = float, 12 = double,
      13 = ifd, 16 = long8, 17 = signed long8, 18 = ifd8
    }
    count: u32be
    room: switch type {
      1 => computed 1 * count
      2 => computed 1 * count
      3 => computed 2 * count
      4 => computed 4 * count
      5 => computed 8 * count
      6 => computed 1 * count
      7 => computed 1 * count
      8 => computed 2 * count
      9 => computed 4 * count
      10 => computed 8 * count
      11 => computed 4 * count
      12 => computed 8 * count
      13 => computed 4 * count
      16 => computed 8 * count
      17 => computed 8 * count
      18 => computed 8 * count
      _ => computed 0
    }
    value: switch tag {
      0x014a => switch count {
        1 => SubIfd (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          directory: at(offset from window) tiff.Ifd.be
        }
        _ => switch 1 < count and (type == 4 or type == 13) {
          1 => SubIfds (inline) {
            offset: u32be
            values: at(offset from window) u32be[count]
            directories: (at(values[index] from window) tiff.Ifd.be)[count]
          }
          _ => switch room / 5 {
            0 => sized(4) Here (inline) {
              values: switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16be, _ => u16be[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8be, _ => i8be[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16be, _ => i16be[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32be, _ => i32be[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32be, _ => f32be[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64be, _ => f64be[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64be, _ => i64be[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
              padding: bytes[remaining]
            }
            _ => Elsewhere (inline) {
              offset: u32be
              values: at(offset from window) switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16be, _ => u16be[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8be, _ => i8be[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16be, _ => i16be[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32be, _ => i32be[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32be, _ => f32be[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64be, _ => f64be[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64be, _ => i64be[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      0x8769 => switch count {
        1 => SubIfd (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          directory: at(offset from window) tiff.ExifIfd.be
        }
        _ => switch 1 < count and (type == 4 or type == 13) {
          1 => SubIfds (inline) {
            offset: u32be
            values: at(offset from window) u32be[count]
            directories: (at(values[index] from window) tiff.ExifIfd.be)[count]
          }
          _ => switch room / 5 {
            0 => sized(4) Here (inline) {
              values: switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16be, _ => u16be[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8be, _ => i8be[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16be, _ => i16be[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32be, _ => i32be[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32be, _ => f32be[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64be, _ => f64be[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64be, _ => i64be[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
              padding: bytes[remaining]
            }
            _ => Elsewhere (inline) {
              offset: u32be
              values: at(offset from window) switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16be, _ => u16be[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8be, _ => i8be[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16be, _ => i16be[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32be, _ => i32be[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32be, _ => f32be[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64be, _ => f64be[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64be, _ => i64be[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      0x8825 => switch count {
        1 => SubIfd (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          directory: at(offset from window) tiff.GpsIfd.be
        }
        _ => switch 1 < count and (type == 4 or type == 13) {
          1 => SubIfds (inline) {
            offset: u32be
            values: at(offset from window) u32be[count]
            directories: (at(values[index] from window) tiff.GpsIfd.be)[count]
          }
          _ => switch room / 5 {
            0 => sized(4) Here (inline) {
              values: switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16be, _ => u16be[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8be, _ => i8be[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16be, _ => i16be[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32be, _ => i32be[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32be, _ => f32be[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64be, _ => f64be[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64be, _ => i64be[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
              padding: bytes[remaining]
            }
            _ => Elsewhere (inline) {
              offset: u32be
              values: at(offset from window) switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16be, _ => u16be[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32be
                    denominator: u32be
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8be, _ => i8be[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16be, _ => i16be[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32be, _ => i32be[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32be
                    denominator: i32be
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32be, _ => f32be[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64be, _ => f64be[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64be, _ => i64be[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      _ => switch room / 5 {
        0 => sized(4) Here (inline) {
          values: switch type {
            2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
            7 => bytes[count]
            1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
            3 => switch count {1 => u16be, _ => u16be[count]}
            4 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
            5 => switch count {
              1 => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: u32be
                denominator: u32be
              }
              _ => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: u32be
                denominator: u32be
              }[count]
            }
            6 => switch count {1 => i8be, _ => i8be[count]}
            8 => switch count {1 => i16be, _ => i16be[count]}
            9 => switch count {1 => i32be, _ => i32be[count]}
            10 => switch count {
              1 => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: i32be
                denominator: i32be
              }
              _ => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: i32be
                denominator: i32be
              }[count]
            }
            11 => switch count {1 => f32be, _ => f32be[count]}
            12 => switch count {1 => f64be, _ => f64be[count]}
            13 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
            16 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
            17 => switch count {1 => i64be, _ => i64be[count]}
            18 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
            _ => bytes[remaining]
          }
          padding: bytes[remaining]
        }
        _ => Elsewhere (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          values: at(offset from window) switch type {
            2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
            7 => bytes[count]
            1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
            3 => switch count {1 => u16be, _ => u16be[count]}
            4 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
            5 => switch count {
              1 => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: u32be
                denominator: u32be
              }
              _ => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: u32be
                denominator: u32be
              }[count]
            }
            6 => switch count {1 => i8be, _ => i8be[count]}
            8 => switch count {1 => i16be, _ => i16be[count]}
            9 => switch count {1 => i32be, _ => i32be[count]}
            10 => switch count {
              1 => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: i32be
                denominator: i32be
              }
              _ => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: i32be
                denominator: i32be
              }[count]
            }
            11 => switch count {1 => f32be, _ => f32be[count]}
            12 => switch count {1 => f64be, _ => f64be[count]}
            13 => switch count {1 => u32be, _ => u32be[count]}
            16 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
            17 => switch count {1 => i64be, _ => i64be[count]}
            18 => switch count {1 => u64be, _ => u64be[count]}
            _ => bytes[remaining]
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }[count]
  next_ifd_offset: u32be
  // The same bytes as StripByteCounts (279), read a second time as a list. Each strip's length
      is looked up here by the strip's number.
  strip_byte_counts: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0117].tag == 279) switch
      entries[tag = 0x0117].room / 5 {
    0 => at(start of entries[tag = 0x0117].value from origin) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0117].type {
        3 => u16be[entries[tag = 0x0117].count]
        4 => u32be[entries[tag = 0x0117].count]
        _ => u32be[0]
      }
    }
    _ => at(entries[tag = 0x0117].value.offset from window) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0117].type {
        3 => u16be[entries[tag = 0x0117].count]
        4 => u32be[entries[tag = 0x0117].count]
        _ => u32be[0]
      }
    }
  }  aside
  // The same bytes as StripOffsets (273), read a second time as a list. Each element holds a
      strip's offset and, beside it, that strip's length from StripByteCounts (279).
  strip_offsets: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0111].tag == 273) switch
      entries[tag = 0x0111].room / 5 {
    0 => at(start of entries[tag = 0x0111].value from origin) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0111].type {
        3 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u16be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0111].count]
        4 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0111].count]
        _ => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[0]
      }
    }
    _ => at(entries[tag = 0x0111].value.offset from window) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0111].type {
        3 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u16be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0111].count]
        4 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0111].count]
        _ => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[0]
      }
    }
  }  aside
  // The image data, split into strips of whole rows. Strip n starts at value n of StripOffsets
      (273), counted from the start of the TIFF, and its length is value n of StripByteCounts
      (279). Strips are numbered from 0. A strip whose offset is past the end of the TIFF is
      left out.
  strips: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0111].tag == 273 and entries[tag = 0x0117].tag == 279)
      gather(from strip_offsets.values[], at offset from window)
      sized(min(descriptor.length, remaining)) bytes[remaining]
      element name from words("strip {}", descriptor.(index))
  // The same bytes as TileByteCounts (325), read a second time as a list. Each tile's length is
      looked up here by the tile's number.
  tile_byte_counts: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0145].tag == 325) switch
      entries[tag = 0x0145].room / 5 {
    0 => at(start of entries[tag = 0x0145].value from origin) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0145].type {
        3 => u16be[entries[tag = 0x0145].count]
        4 => u32be[entries[tag = 0x0145].count]
        _ => u32be[0]
      }
    }
    _ => at(entries[tag = 0x0145].value.offset from window) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0145].type {
        3 => u16be[entries[tag = 0x0145].count]
        4 => u32be[entries[tag = 0x0145].count]
        _ => u32be[0]
      }
    }
  }  aside
  // The same bytes as TileOffsets (324), read a second time as a list. Each element holds a
      tile's offset and, beside it, that tile's length from TileByteCounts (325).
  tile_offsets: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0144].tag == 324) switch
      entries[tag = 0x0144].room / 5 {
    0 => at(start of entries[tag = 0x0144].value from origin) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0144].type {
        3 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u16be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0144].count]
        4 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0144].count]
        _ => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[0]
      }
    }
    _ => at(entries[tag = 0x0144].value.offset from window) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0144].type {
        3 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u16be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0144].count]
        4 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0144].count]
        _ => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32be
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[0]
      }
    }
  }  aside
  // The image data, split into tiles. Tile n starts at value n of TileOffsets (324), counted
      from the start of the TIFF, and its length is value n of TileByteCounts (325). Tiles are
      numbered from 0, left to right and then top to bottom. A tile whose offset is past the end
      of the TIFF is left out.
  tiles: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0144].tag == 324 and entries[tag = 0x0145].tag == 325)
      gather(from tile_offsets.values[], at offset from window)
      sized(min(descriptor.length, remaining)) bytes[remaining]
      element name from words("tile {}", descriptor.(index))
  // The JPEG image at the offset in JPEGInterchangeFormat (513), counted from the start of the
      TIFF, with the length in JPEGInterchangeFormatLength (514). In IFD 1 of an EXIF block,
      this is the thumbnail. In a raw file, it can be a full-size preview.
  thumbnail: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0201].tag == 513 and entries[tag = 0x0201].count == 1
      and (entries[tag = 0x0202].tag == 514 and entries[tag = 0x0202].count == 1) and
      entries[tag = 0x0201].value.values > 0) optional(when entries[tag = 0x0201].value.values <
      size of window) at(entries[tag = 0x0201].value.values from window)
      sized(min(entries[tag = 0x0202].value.values, size of window -
      entries[tag = 0x0201].value.values)) switch 1 < remaining and peek(u16be) == 65496 {
    1 => tiff.Jpeg
    _ => bytes[remaining]
  }
}

type tiff.Ifd.le = Ifd (contents entries, machinery strip_offsets strip_byte_counts tile_offsets
    tile_byte_counts) {
  count: u16le
  entries: Entry (named by tag, unit entry) {
    tag: u16le enum Tag {
      254 = new subfile type, 255 = subfile type, 256 = image width, 257 = image height,
      258 = bits per sample, 259 = compression, 262 = photometric interpretation,
      263 = thresholding, 266 = fill order, 269 = document name, 270 = image description,
      271 = make, 272 = model, 273 = strip offsets, 274 = orientation, 277 = samples per pixel,
      278 = rows per strip, 279 = strip byte counts, 280 = min sample value,
      281 = max sample value, 282 = x resolution, 283 = y resolution,
      284 = planar configuration, 288 = free offsets, 289 = free byte counts,
      290 = grey response unit, 291 = grey response curve, 296 = resolution unit,
      297 = page number, 305 = software, 306 = date time, 315 = artist, 316 = host computer,
      317 = predictor, 318 = white point, 319 = primary chromaticities, 320 = colour map,
      321 = halftone hints, 322 = tile width, 323 = tile length, 324 = tile offsets,
      325 = tile byte counts, 330 = sub ifds, 338 = extra samples, 339 = sample format,
      513 = jpeg interchange format, 514 = jpeg interchange format length,
      529 = ycbcr coefficients, 530 = ycbcr subsampling, 531 = ycbcr positioning,
      532 = reference black white, 700 = xmp, 33421 = cfa repeat pattern dimensions,
      33422 = cfa pattern, 33432 = copyright, 33723 = iptc, 34665 = exif ifd,
      34675 = icc profile, 34713 = nikon nef info, 34853 = gps ifd,
      50341 = print image matching, 37398 = tiff/ep standard id, 50706 = dng version,
      50707 = dng backward version, 50708 = unique camera model,
      50709 = localised camera model, 50710 = cfa plane colour, 50711 = cfa layout,
      50712 = linearisation table, 50713 = black level repeat dimensions, 50714 = black level,
      50715 = black level delta h, 50716 = black level delta v, 50717 = white level,
      50718 = default scale, 50719 = default crop origin, 50720 = default crop size,
      50721 = colour matrix 1, 50722 = colour matrix 2, 50723 = camera calibration 1,
      50724 = camera calibration 2, 50725 = reduction matrix 1, 50726 = reduction matrix 2,
      50727 = analogue balance, 50728 = as shot neutral, 50729 = as shot white xy,
      50730 = baseline exposure, 50731 = baseline noise, 50732 = baseline sharpness,
      50733 = bayer green split, 50734 = linear response limit, 50735 = camera serial number,
      50736 = lens info, 50737 = chroma blur radius, 50738 = anti alias strength,
      50739 = shadow scale, 50740 = dng private data, 50741 = maker note safety,
      50778 = calibration illuminant 1, 50779 = calibration illuminant 2,
      50780 = best quality scale, 50781 = raw data unique id, 50827 = original raw file name,
      50828 = original raw file data, 50829 = active area, 50830 = masked areas,
      50831 = as shot icc profile, 50832 = as shot pre-profile matrix,
      50833 = current icc profile, 50834 = current pre-profile matrix
    }
    type: u16le enum FieldType {
      1 = byte, 2 = ascii, 3 = short, 4 = long, 5 = rational, 6 = signed byte, 7 = undefined,
      8 = signed short, 9 = signed long, 10 = signed rational, 11 = float, 12 = double,
      13 = ifd, 16 = long8, 17 = signed long8, 18 = ifd8
    }
    count: u32le
    room: switch type {
      1 => computed 1 * count
      2 => computed 1 * count
      3 => computed 2 * count
      4 => computed 4 * count
      5 => computed 8 * count
      6 => computed 1 * count
      7 => computed 1 * count
      8 => computed 2 * count
      9 => computed 4 * count
      10 => computed 8 * count
      11 => computed 4 * count
      12 => computed 8 * count
      13 => computed 4 * count
      16 => computed 8 * count
      17 => computed 8 * count
      18 => computed 8 * count
      _ => computed 0
    }
    value: switch tag {
      0x014a => switch count {
        1 => SubIfd (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          directory: at(offset from window) tiff.Ifd.le
        }
        _ => switch 1 < count and (type == 4 or type == 13) {
          1 => SubIfds (inline) {
            offset: u32le
            values: at(offset from window) u32le[count]
            directories: (at(values[index] from window) tiff.Ifd.le)[count]
          }
          _ => switch room / 5 {
            0 => sized(4) Here (inline) {
              values: switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16le, _ => u16le[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8le, _ => i8le[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16le, _ => i16le[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32le, _ => i32le[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32le, _ => f32le[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64le, _ => f64le[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64le, _ => i64le[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
              padding: bytes[remaining]
            }
            _ => Elsewhere (inline) {
              offset: u32le
              values: at(offset from window) switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16le, _ => u16le[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8le, _ => i8le[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16le, _ => i16le[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32le, _ => i32le[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32le, _ => f32le[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64le, _ => f64le[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64le, _ => i64le[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      0x8769 => switch count {
        1 => SubIfd (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          directory: at(offset from window) tiff.ExifIfd.le
        }
        _ => switch 1 < count and (type == 4 or type == 13) {
          1 => SubIfds (inline) {
            offset: u32le
            values: at(offset from window) u32le[count]
            directories: (at(values[index] from window) tiff.ExifIfd.le)[count]
          }
          _ => switch room / 5 {
            0 => sized(4) Here (inline) {
              values: switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16le, _ => u16le[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8le, _ => i8le[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16le, _ => i16le[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32le, _ => i32le[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32le, _ => f32le[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64le, _ => f64le[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64le, _ => i64le[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
              padding: bytes[remaining]
            }
            _ => Elsewhere (inline) {
              offset: u32le
              values: at(offset from window) switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16le, _ => u16le[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8le, _ => i8le[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16le, _ => i16le[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32le, _ => i32le[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32le, _ => f32le[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64le, _ => f64le[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64le, _ => i64le[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      0x8825 => switch count {
        1 => SubIfd (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          directory: at(offset from window) tiff.GpsIfd.le
        }
        _ => switch 1 < count and (type == 4 or type == 13) {
          1 => SubIfds (inline) {
            offset: u32le
            values: at(offset from window) u32le[count]
            directories: (at(values[index] from window) tiff.GpsIfd.le)[count]
          }
          _ => switch room / 5 {
            0 => sized(4) Here (inline) {
              values: switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16le, _ => u16le[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8le, _ => i8le[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16le, _ => i16le[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32le, _ => i32le[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32le, _ => f32le[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64le, _ => f64le[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64le, _ => i64le[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
              padding: bytes[remaining]
            }
            _ => Elsewhere (inline) {
              offset: u32le
              values: at(offset from window) switch type {
                2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
                7 => bytes[count]
                1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
                3 => switch count {1 => u16le, _ => u16le[count]}
                4 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                5 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: u32le
                    denominator: u32le
                  }[count]
                }
                6 => switch count {1 => i8le, _ => i8le[count]}
                8 => switch count {1 => i16le, _ => i16le[count]}
                9 => switch count {1 => i32le, _ => i32le[count]}
                10 => switch count {
                  1 => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }
                  _ => Rational (inline) {
                    numerator: i32le
                    denominator: i32le
                  }[count]
                }
                11 => switch count {1 => f32le, _ => f32le[count]}
                12 => switch count {1 => f64le, _ => f64le[count]}
                13 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
                16 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                17 => switch count {1 => i64le, _ => i64le[count]}
                18 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      _ => switch room / 5 {
        0 => sized(4) Here (inline) {
          values: switch type {
            2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
            7 => bytes[count]
            1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
            3 => switch count {1 => u16le, _ => u16le[count]}
            4 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
            5 => switch count {
              1 => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: u32le
                denominator: u32le
              }
              _ => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: u32le
                denominator: u32le
              }[count]
            }
            6 => switch count {1 => i8le, _ => i8le[count]}
            8 => switch count {1 => i16le, _ => i16le[count]}
            9 => switch count {1 => i32le, _ => i32le[count]}
            10 => switch count {
              1 => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: i32le
                denominator: i32le
              }
              _ => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: i32le
                denominator: i32le
              }[count]
            }
            11 => switch count {1 => f32le, _ => f32le[count]}
            12 => switch count {1 => f64le, _ => f64le[count]}
            13 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
            16 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
            17 => switch count {1 => i64le, _ => i64le[count]}
            18 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
            _ => bytes[remaining]
          }
          padding: bytes[remaining]
        }
        _ => Elsewhere (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          values: at(offset from window) switch type {
            2 => text[count] padded with 0x00 ascii
            7 => bytes[count]
            1 => switch count {1 => u8be, _ => u8be[count]}
            3 => switch count {1 => u16le, _ => u16le[count]}
            4 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
            5 => switch count {
              1 => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: u32le
                denominator: u32le
              }
              _ => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: u32le
                denominator: u32le
              }[count]
            }
            6 => switch count {1 => i8le, _ => i8le[count]}
            8 => switch count {1 => i16le, _ => i16le[count]}
            9 => switch count {1 => i32le, _ => i32le[count]}
            10 => switch count {
              1 => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: i32le
                denominator: i32le
              }
              _ => Rational (inline) {
                numerator: i32le
                denominator: i32le
              }[count]
            }
            11 => switch count {1 => f32le, _ => f32le[count]}
            12 => switch count {1 => f64le, _ => f64le[count]}
            13 => switch count {1 => u32le, _ => u32le[count]}
            16 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
            17 => switch count {1 => i64le, _ => i64le[count]}
            18 => switch count {1 => u64le, _ => u64le[count]}
            _ => bytes[remaining]
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }[count]
  next_ifd_offset: u32le
  // The same bytes as StripByteCounts (279), read a second time as a list. Each strip's length
      is looked up here by the strip's number.
  strip_byte_counts: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0117].tag == 279) switch
      entries[tag = 0x0117].room / 5 {
    0 => at(start of entries[tag = 0x0117].value from origin) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0117].type {
        3 => u16le[entries[tag = 0x0117].count]
        4 => u32le[entries[tag = 0x0117].count]
        _ => u32le[0]
      }
    }
    _ => at(entries[tag = 0x0117].value.offset from window) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0117].type {
        3 => u16le[entries[tag = 0x0117].count]
        4 => u32le[entries[tag = 0x0117].count]
        _ => u32le[0]
      }
    }
  }  aside
  // The same bytes as StripOffsets (273), read a second time as a list. Each element holds a
      strip's offset and, beside it, that strip's length from StripByteCounts (279).
  strip_offsets: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0111].tag == 273) switch
      entries[tag = 0x0111].room / 5 {
    0 => at(start of entries[tag = 0x0111].value from origin) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0111].type {
        3 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u16le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0111].count]
        4 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0111].count]
        _ => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[0]
      }
    }
    _ => at(entries[tag = 0x0111].value.offset from window) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0111].type {
        3 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u16le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0111].count]
        4 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0111].count]
        _ => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0117].count ? strip_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[0]
      }
    }
  }  aside
  // The image data, split into strips of whole rows. Strip n starts at value n of StripOffsets
      (273), counted from the start of the TIFF, and its length is value n of StripByteCounts
      (279). Strips are numbered from 0. A strip whose offset is past the end of the TIFF is
      left out.
  strips: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0111].tag == 273 and entries[tag = 0x0117].tag == 279)
      gather(from strip_offsets.values[], at offset from window)
      sized(min(descriptor.length, remaining)) bytes[remaining]
      element name from words("strip {}", descriptor.(index))
  // The same bytes as TileByteCounts (325), read a second time as a list. Each tile's length is
      looked up here by the tile's number.
  tile_byte_counts: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0145].tag == 325) switch
      entries[tag = 0x0145].room / 5 {
    0 => at(start of entries[tag = 0x0145].value from origin) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0145].type {
        3 => u16le[entries[tag = 0x0145].count]
        4 => u32le[entries[tag = 0x0145].count]
        _ => u32le[0]
      }
    }
    _ => at(entries[tag = 0x0145].value.offset from window) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0145].type {
        3 => u16le[entries[tag = 0x0145].count]
        4 => u32le[entries[tag = 0x0145].count]
        _ => u32le[0]
      }
    }
  }  aside
  // The same bytes as TileOffsets (324), read a second time as a list. Each element holds a
      tile's offset and, beside it, that tile's length from TileByteCounts (325).
  tile_offsets: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0144].tag == 324) switch
      entries[tag = 0x0144].room / 5 {
    0 => at(start of entries[tag = 0x0144].value from origin) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0144].type {
        3 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u16le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0144].count]
        4 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0144].count]
        _ => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[0]
      }
    }
    _ => at(entries[tag = 0x0144].value.offset from window) Numbers (inline) {
      values: switch entries[tag = 0x0144].type {
        3 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u16le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0144].count]
        4 => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[entries[tag = 0x0144].count]
        _ => Piece (inline) {
          offset: u32le
          length: computed index < entries[tag = 0x0145].count ? tile_byte_counts.values[index]
              : 0
        }[0]
      }
    }
  }  aside
  // The image data, split into tiles. Tile n starts at value n of TileOffsets (324), counted
      from the start of the TIFF, and its length is value n of TileByteCounts (325). Tiles are
      numbered from 0, left to right and then top to bottom. A tile whose offset is past the end
      of the TIFF is left out.
  tiles: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0144].tag == 324 and entries[tag = 0x0145].tag == 325)
      gather(from tile_offsets.values[], at offset from window)
      sized(min(descriptor.length, remaining)) bytes[remaining]
      element name from words("tile {}", descriptor.(index))
  // The JPEG image at the offset in JPEGInterchangeFormat (513), counted from the start of the
      TIFF, with the length in JPEGInterchangeFormatLength (514). In IFD 1 of an EXIF block,
      this is the thumbnail. In a raw file, it can be a full-size preview.
  thumbnail: optional(when entries[tag = 0x0201].tag == 513 and entries[tag = 0x0201].count == 1
      and (entries[tag = 0x0202].tag == 514 and entries[tag = 0x0202].count == 1) and
      entries[tag = 0x0201].value.values > 0) optional(when entries[tag = 0x0201].value.values <
      size of window) at(entries[tag = 0x0201].value.values from window)
      sized(min(entries[tag = 0x0202].value.values, size of window -
      entries[tag = 0x0201].value.values)) switch 1 < remaining and peek(u16be) == 65496 {
    1 => tiff.Jpeg
    _ => bytes[remaining]
  }
}

type tiff.Jpeg = JPEG {
  soi: magic FF D8
  segments: repeat(until element.marker is FF D9) Segment
      (named by marker, contents body, unit segment) {
    marker: u16be enum Marker {
      0xffd8 = soi, start of image, 0xffc0 = sof0, baseline dct,
      0xffc1 = sof1, extended sequential dct, 0xffc2 = sof2, progressive dct,
      0xffc3 = sof3, lossless, 0xffc4 = dht, huffman tables,
      0xffc5 = sof5, differential sequential dct, 0xffc6 = sof6, differential progressive dct,
      0xffc7 = sof7, differential lossless, 0xffc8 = jpg, reserved,
      0xffc9 = sof9, arithmetic sequential dct, 0xffca = sof10, arithmetic progressive dct,
      0xffcb = sof11, arithmetic lossless, 0xffcc = dac, arithmetic coding conditioning,
      0xffcd = sof13, differential arithmetic sequential dct,
      0xffce = sof14, differential arithmetic progressive dct,
      0xffcf = sof15, differential arithmetic lossless, 0xffd0 = rst0, restart,
      0xffd1 = rst1, restart, 0xffd2 = rst2, restart, 0xffd3 = rst3, restart,
      0xffd4 = rst4, restart, 0xffd5 = rst5, restart, 0xffd6 = rst6, restart,
      0xffd7 = rst7, restart, 0xffd9 = eoi, end of image, 0xffda = sos, start of scan,
      0xffdb = dqt, quantisation tables, 0xffdc = dnl, number of lines,
      0xffdd = dri, restart interval, 0xffde = dhp, hierarchical progression,
      0xffdf = exp, expand reference, 0xffe0 = app0, jfif, 0xffe1 = app1, exif or xmp,
      0xffe2 = app2, icc profile, 0xffe3 = app3, 0xffe4 = app4, 0xffe5 = app5, 0xffe6 = app6,
      0xffe7 = app7, 0xffe8 = app8, 0xffe9 = app9, 0xffea = app10, 0xffeb = app11,
      0xffec = app12, picture info, 0xffed = app13, photoshop irb, 0xffee = app14, adobe,
      0xffef = app15, 0xfffe = com, comment, 0xff01 = tem, temporary
    }
    body: switch marker {
      0xffd8 => bytes[0]
      0xffd9 => bytes[0]
      0xff01 => bytes[0]
      0xffd0 => bytes[0]
      0xffd1 => bytes[0]
      0xffd2 => bytes[0]
      0xffd3 => bytes[0]
      0xffd4 => bytes[0]
      0xffd5 => bytes[0]
      0xffd6 => bytes[0]
      0xffd7 => bytes[0]
      0xffc0 => Body (contents contents) {
        length: u16be
        contents: sized(length - 2) Frame (line width worded "width {}" then height worded
            "height {}" then components then precision worded "precision {}" except "8") {
          precision: u8be
          height: u16be
          width: u16be
          component_count: u8be
          components: Component (named by id, unit component, line h_sampling worded "h {}" then
              v_sampling worded "v {}" then quant_table worded "quant table {}") {
            id: u8be
            h_sampling: u4be
            v_sampling: u4be
            quant_table: u8be
          }[component_count]
        }
      }
      0xffc1 => Body (contents contents) {

        contents: sized(length - 2) Frame (line width worded "width {}" then height worded
