                                (grid_points[5] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[5]) *
                                (grid_points[6] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[6]) *
                                (grid_points[7] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[7]) *
                                (grid_points[8] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[8]) *
                                (grid_points[9] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[9]) *
                                (grid_points[10] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[10]) *
                                (grid_points[11] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[11]) *
                                (grid_points[12] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[12]) *
                                (grid_points[13] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[13]) *
                                (grid_points[14] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[14]) *
                                (grid_points[15] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[15]) * output_channels]
                          }
                        }
                        a_curves: optional(when a_curves_offset != 0)
                            at(a_curves_offset from window) Curve
                            (contents contents, unit curve) {
                          type: u32be enum TagType {
                            0x6368726d = chrm, chromaticity,
                            0x63696370 = cicp, coding-independent code points,
                            0x636c726f = clro, colorant order,
                            0x636c7274 = clrt, colorant table, 0x63757276 = curv, curve,
                            0x64617461 = data, ASCII or binary data,
                            0x6474696d = dtim, date and time, 0x64696374 = dict, dictionary,
                            0x6d667432 = mft2, 16-bit lookup table,
                            0x6d667431 = mft1, 8-bit lookup table,
                            0x6d414220 = mAB, A to B lookup, 0x6d424120 = mBA, B to A lookup,
                            0x6d656173 = meas, measurement,
                            0x6d6c7563 = mluc, multi-localized Unicode,
                            0x6d706574 = mpet, multi-process elements,
                            0x6e636c32 = ncl2, named colour 2,
                            0x70617261 = para, parametric curve,
                            0x70736571 = pseq, profile sequence description,
                            0x70736964 = psid, profile sequence identifier,
                            0x72637332 = rcs2, response curve set,
                            0x73663332 = sf32, s15Fixed16 array, 0x73696720 = sig, signature,
                            0x74657874 = text, ASCII text, 0x75663332 = uf32, u16Fixed16 array,
                            0x75693038 = ui08, 8-bit integer array,
                            0x75693136 = ui16, 16-bit integer array,
                            0x75693332 = ui32, 32-bit integer array,
                            0x75693634 = ui64, 64-bit integer array,
                            0x76696577 = view, viewing conditions,
                            0x58595a20 = XYZ, XYZ values, 0x64657363 = desc, text description,
                            0x6e636f6c = ncol, named colour, 0x7363726e = scrn, screening,
                            0x62666420 = bfd, undercolour removal and black generation,
                            0x63726469 = crdi, CRD info, 0x64657673 = devs, device settings,
                            0x76636774 = vcgt, video card gamma (Apple private type)
                          }
                          reserved: u32be
                          contents: switch type {
                            'curv' => curveType {
                              // Number of entries. 0 means the identity curve, and with 1 the
                                  entry is the gamma exponent.
                              count: u32be
                              // The exponent: Y = X^gamma.
                              gamma: optional(when count == 1) u8Fixed8Number
                                  (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                                stored: u16be
                                worth: computed real stored / 256.0
                              }
                              // Y values of the curve at evenly spaced X from 0.0 to 1.0. Each
                                  Y value is stored from 0 for 0.0 to 65535 for 1.0.
                              entries: optional(when count > 1) u16be[count]
                            }
                            'para' => parametricCurveType {
                              function: u16be enum ParametricFunction {
                                0 = Y = X^g,
                                1 = Y = (aX + b)^g for X ≥ -b/a, else 0 (CIE 122-1966),
                                2 = Y = (aX + b)^g + c for X ≥ -b/a, else c (IEC 61966-3),
                                3 = Y = (aX + b)^g for X ≥ d, else cX (IEC 61966-2-1, sRGB),
                                4 = Y = (aX + b)^g + e for X ≥ d, else cX + f
                              }
                              reserved: u16be
                              g: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                                stored: i32be
                                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                              }
                              a: optional(when function >= 1 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                                  (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                                stored: i32be
                                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                              }
                              b: optional(when function >= 1 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                                  (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                                stored: i32be
                                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                              }
                              c: optional(when function >= 2 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                                  (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                                stored: i32be
                                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                              }
                              d: optional(when function >= 3 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                                  (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                                stored: i32be
                                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                              }
                              e: optional(when function >= 4 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                                  (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                                stored: i32be
                                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                              }
                              f: optional(when function >= 4 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                                  (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                                stored: i32be
                                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                              }
                            }
                            _ => bytes[0]
                          }
                          padding: u(min(padding(sizeof(type) + sizeof(reserved) +
                              sizeof(contents), 4), remaining) * 8)be
                        }[output_channels]
                      }
                      'dict' => dictType {
                        record_count: u32be
                        // Bytes in each record: 16, 24 or 32.
                        record_length: u32be
                        records: NameValue (named by name, unit entry) {
                          name_offset: u32be
                          name_size: u32be
                          value_offset: u32be
                          value_size: u32be
                          display_name_offset: optional(when record_length >= 24) u32be
                          display_name_size: optional(when record_length >= 24) u32be
                          display_value_offset: optional(when record_length >= 32) u32be
                          display_value_size: optional(when record_length >= 32) u32be
                          name: optional(when name_offset != 0) at(name_offset from window)
                              text[name_size] utf16be
                          value: optional(when value_offset != 0) at(value_offset from window)
                              text[value_size] utf16be
                          display_name: optional(when record_length >= 24)
                              optional(when display_name_offset != 0 and display_name_size != 0)
                              at(display_name_offset from window) sized(display_name_size)
                              DisplayText (contents contents) {
                            type: u32be enum TagType {
                              0x6368726d = chrm, chromaticity,
                              0x63696370 = cicp, coding-independent code points,
                              0x636c726f = clro, colorant order,
                              0x636c7274 = clrt, colorant table, 0x63757276 = curv, curve,
                              0x64617461 = data, ASCII or binary data,
                              0x6474696d = dtim, date and time, 0x64696374 = dict, dictionary,
                              0x6d667432 = mft2, 16-bit lookup table,
                              0x6d667431 = mft1, 8-bit lookup table,
                              0x6d414220 = mAB, A to B lookup, 0x6d424120 = mBA, B to A lookup,
                              0x6d656173 = meas, measurement,
                              0x6d6c7563 = mluc, multi-localized Unicode,
                              0x6d706574 = mpet, multi-process elements,
                              0x6e636c32 = ncl2, named colour 2,
                              0x70617261 = para, parametric curve,
                              0x70736571 = pseq, profile sequence description,
                              0x70736964 = psid, profile sequence identifier,
                              0x72637332 = rcs2, response curve set,
                              0x73663332 = sf32, s15Fixed16 array, 0x73696720 = sig, signature,
                              0x74657874 = text, ASCII text,
                              0x75663332 = uf32, u16Fixed16 array,
                              0x75693038 = ui08, 8-bit integer array,
                              0x75693136 = ui16, 16-bit integer array,
                              0x75693332 = ui32, 32-bit integer array,
                              0x75693634 = ui64, 64-bit integer array,
                              0x76696577 = view, viewing conditions,
                              0x58595a20 = XYZ, XYZ values,
                              0x64657363 = desc, text description,
                              0x6e636f6c = ncol, named colour, 0x7363726e = scrn, screening,
                              0x62666420 = bfd, undercolour removal and black generation,
                              0x63726469 = crdi, CRD info, 0x64657673 = devs, device settings,
                              0x76636774 = vcgt, video card gamma (Apple private type)
                            }
                            reserved: u32be
                            contents: switch type {
                              'mluc' => multiLocalizedUnicodeType {
                                record_count: u32be
                                // Bytes per record: 12.
                                record_size: u32be
                                records: LocalizedString (contents string, unit string) {
                                  language: text[2] padded with 0x00 ascii
                                  country: text[2] padded with 0x00 ascii
                                  length: u32be
                                  offset: u32be
                                  string: at(offset from window) text[length] utf16be
                                }[record_count]
                              }
                              _ => bytes[remaining]
                            }
                          }
                          display_value: optional(when record_length >= 32)
                              optional(when display_value_offset != 0 and display_value_size !=
                              0) at(display_value_offset from window) sized(display_value_size)
                              DisplayText (contents contents) {
                            type: u32be enum TagType {
                              0x6368726d = chrm, chromaticity,
                              0x63696370 = cicp, coding-independent code points,
                              0x636c726f = clro, colorant order,
                              0x636c7274 = clrt, colorant table, 0x63757276 = curv, curve,
                              0x64617461 = data, ASCII or binary data,
                              0x6474696d = dtim, date and time, 0x64696374 = dict, dictionary,
                              0x6d667432 = mft2, 16-bit lookup table,
                              0x6d667431 = mft1, 8-bit lookup table,
                              0x6d414220 = mAB, A to B lookup, 0x6d424120 = mBA, B to A lookup,
                              0x6d656173 = meas, measurement,
                              0x6d6c7563 = mluc, multi-localized Unicode,
                              0x6d706574 = mpet, multi-process elements,
                              0x6e636c32 = ncl2, named colour 2,
                              0x70617261 = para, parametric curve,
                              0x70736571 = pseq, profile sequence description,
                              0x70736964 = psid, profile sequence identifier,
                              0x72637332 = rcs2, response curve set,
                              0x73663332 = sf32, s15Fixed16 array, 0x73696720 = sig, signature,
                              0x74657874 = text, ASCII text,
                              0x75663332 = uf32, u16Fixed16 array,
                              0x75693038 = ui08, 8-bit integer array,
                              0x75693136 = ui16, 16-bit integer array,
                              0x75693332 = ui32, 32-bit integer array,
                              0x75693634 = ui64, 64-bit integer array,
                              0x76696577 = view, viewing conditions,
                              0x58595a20 = XYZ, XYZ values,
                              0x64657363 = desc, text description,
                              0x6e636f6c = ncol, named colour, 0x7363726e = scrn, screening,
                              0x62666420 = bfd, undercolour removal and black generation,
                              0x63726469 = crdi, CRD info, 0x64657673 = devs, device settings,
                              0x76636774 = vcgt, video card gamma (Apple private type)
                            }
                            reserved: u32be
                            contents: switch type {
                              'mluc' => multiLocalizedUnicodeType {
                                record_count: u32be
                                // Bytes per record: 12.
                                record_size: u32be
                                records: LocalizedString (contents string, unit string) {
                                  language: text[2] padded with 0x00 ascii
                                  country: text[2] padded with 0x00 ascii
                                  length: u32be
                                  offset: u32be
                                  string: at(offset from window) text[length] utf16be
                                }[record_count]
                              }
                              _ => bytes[remaining]
                            }
                          }
                        }[record_count]
                      }
                      'vcgt' => vcgtType {
                        form: u32be enum VideoCardGammaForm {0 = table, 1 = formula}
                        gamma: switch form {
                          0 => VideoCardGammaTable {
                            channels: u16be
                            entry_count: u16be
                            // Bytes per entry: 1 or 2.
                            entry_size: u16be
                            ramps: (switch entry_size {
                              1 => u8be
                              _ => u16be
                            }[entry_count])[channels]
                          }
                          1 => VideoCardGammaFormula {
                            red_gamma: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                              stored: i32be
                              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                            }
                            red_min: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                              stored: i32be
                              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                            }
                            red_max: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                              stored: i32be
                              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                            }
                            green_gamma: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                              stored: i32be
                              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                            }
                            green_min: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                              stored: i32be
                              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                            }
                            green_max: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                              stored: i32be
                              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                            }
                            blue_gamma: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                              stored: i32be
                              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                            }
                            blue_min: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                              stored: i32be
                              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                            }
                            blue_max: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                              stored: i32be
                              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                            }
                          }
                          _ => bytes[remaining]
                        }
                      }
                      _ => bytes[remaining]
                    }
                    // Bytes after the tag's contents that the tag's size still counts, usually
                        padding to a four-byte boundary.
                    padding: switch type {
                      'mluc' => bytes[0]
                      'dict' => bytes[0]
                      'mAB ' => bytes[0]
                      'mBA ' => bytes[0]
                      _ => bytes[remaining]
                    }
                  }
                }[tag_count]
              }
              // This segment's piece of a profile split across several APP2 segments. The whole
                  profile is read in icc_profile, from all the pieces joined in order.
              piece: optional(when 1 < piece_count) bytes[remaining]
            }
            _ => bytes[remaining]
          }
        }
      }
      0xffed => Body (contents contents) {
        length: u16be
        contents: sized(length - 2) App13 (contents data) {
          identifier: text until 0x00 utf8
          data: match identifier {
            "Photoshop 3.0" => repeat(until end) ImageResource
                (named by resource_id, contents data, unit resource) {
              signature: text[4] ascii
              resource_id: u16be enum ImageResourceId {
                1005 = resolution info, 1028 = IPTC-NAA, 1032 = grid and guides,
                1033 = thumbnail (Photoshop 4), 1036 = thumbnail, 1039 = ICC profile,
                1045 = Unicode alpha names, 1058 = EXIF data 1, 1059 = EXIF data 3,
                1060 = XMP metadata, 1061 = caption digest, 1062 = print scale,
                1069 = layer selection IDs, 1072 = layer group enabled IDs,
                1082 = print information, 1083 = print style, 1086 = pixel aspect ratio
              }
              name_length: u8be
              name: text[name_length] latin1
              name_pad: bytes[(name_length + 1 + 1) / 2 * 2 - (name_length + 1)]
              data_size: u32be
              data: sized(data_size) switch resource_id {
                0x03ed => ResolutionInfo {
                  h_res_fixed: u32be
                  // Horizontal resolution in pixels per inch: h_res_fixed divided by 65,536.
                  h_res: computed real h_res_fixed / 65536.0
                  // The unit Photoshop shows the horizontal resolution in. The stored value is
                      pixels per inch either way.
                  h_res_unit: u16be enum ResolutionUnit {1 = pixels per inch, 2 = pixels per cm}
                  width_unit: u16be enum SizeUnit {
                    1 = inches, 2 = cm, 3 = points, 4 = picas, 5 = columns
                  }
                  v_res_fixed: u32be
                  // Vertical resolution in pixels per inch: v_res_fixed divided by 65,536.
                  v_res: computed real v_res_fixed / 65536.0
                  // The unit Photoshop shows the vertical resolution in. The stored value is
                      pixels per inch either way.
                  v_res_unit: u16be enum ResolutionUnit {1 = pixels per inch, 2 = pixels per cm}
                  height_unit: u16be enum SizeUnit {
                    1 = inches, 2 = cm, 3 = points, 4 = picas, 5 = columns
                  }
                }
                0x0404 => repeat(until end) IptcDataset (named by dataset, unit dataset) {
                  marker: magic 1C
                  // Which IPTC record the dataset belongs to: 1 is the envelope, 2 the
                      application record that holds captions, keywords and credits.
                  record: u8be
                  dataset: switch record {
                    1 => u8be enum IptcEnvelopeDataset {
                      0 = record version, 90 = coded character set
                    }
                    2 => u8be enum IptcApplicationDataset {
                      0 = record version, 5 = object name, 7 = edit status, 10 = urgency,
                      15 = category, 20 = supplemental category, 25 = keywords,
                      40 = special instructions, 55 = date created, 60 = time created,
                      62 = digital creation date, 63 = digital creation time,
                      65 = originating program, 70 = program version, 80 = by-line,
                      85 = by-line title, 90 = city, 92 = sub-location, 95 = province or state,
                      100 = country code, 101 = country name,
                      103 = original transmission reference, 105 = headline, 110 = credit,
                      115 = source, 116 = copyright notice, 118 = contact, 120 = caption,
                      122 = caption writer
                    }
                    _ => u8be
                  }
                  size: u16be
                  data: switch record * 256 + dataset {
                    0x0200 => u16be
                    0x0100 => u16be
                    _ => switch record {2 => text[size] utf8, _ => bytes[size]}
                  }
                }
                0x0424 => text[remaining] utf8
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
              data_pad: bytes[data_size - data_size / 2 * 2]
            }
            _ => bytes[remaining]
          }
        }
      }
      0xffee => Body (contents contents) {
        length: u16be
        contents: sized(length - 2) App14 (line identifier except "Adobe" then transform) {
          identifier: text[5] latin1
          version: u16be
          flags0: u16be
          flags1: u16be
          transform: u8be enum AdobeTransform {0 = no transform, 1 = ycbcr, 2 = ycck}
          // Bytes after the transform flag. Their meaning is not published, and decoders do not
              read them.
          extra: bytes[remaining]
        }
      }
      0xffe0 => Body (contents contents) {
        length: u16be
        contents: sized(length - 2) App0 (contents data, line identifier except "JFIF" then
            data) {
          identifier: text until 0x00 utf8
          data: match identifier {
            "JFIF" => Jfif (line x_density worded "x {}" then y_density worded "y {}" then
                density_units then thumbnail worded "thumbnail {}" except "0 pixels") {
              version_major: u8be
              version_minor: u8be
              density_units: u8be enum DensityUnits {
                0 = none, aspect ratio only, 1 = per inch, 2 = per centimetre
              }
              x_density: u16be
              y_density: u16be
              thumbnail_width: u8be
              thumbnail_height: u8be
              thumbnail: RgbPixel (unit pixel, colour red green blue) {
                red: u8be
                green: u8be
                blue: u8be
              }[thumbnail_width * thumbnail_height]
            }
            "JFXX" => Jfxx {
              // The thumbnail's format: 0x10 is a JPEG, 0x11 is a 256-colour palette followed
                  by one palette index per pixel, 0x13 is three bytes of RGB per pixel.
              extension_code: u8be enum JfxxExtension {
                16 = JPEG thumbnail, 17 = palette thumbnail, 19 = RGB thumbnail
              }
              thumbnail: switch extension_code {
                17 => PaletteThumbnail {
                  thumbnail_width: u8be
                  thumbnail_height: u8be
                  palette: PaletteEntry (unit entry, colour red green blue) {
                    red: u8be
                    green: u8be
                    blue: u8be
                  }[256]
                  pixels: u8be[thumbnail_width * thumbnail_height]
                }
                19 => RgbThumbnail {
                  thumbnail_width: u8be
                  thumbnail_height: u8be
                  pixels: RgbPixel (unit pixel, colour red green blue) {
                    red: u8be
                    green: u8be
                    blue: u8be
                  }[thumbnail_width * thumbnail_height]
                }
                _ => bytes[remaining]
              }
            }
            _ => bytes[remaining]
          }
        }
      }
      0xffe1 => Body (contents contents) {
        length: u16be
        contents: sized(length - 2) App1 (contents data) {
          identifier: text until 0x00 utf8
          data: match identifier {
            "Exif" => Exif {
              pad: magic 00
              tiff: sized(remaining) TIFF (contents file) {
                byte_order: u16be enum ByteOrder {
                  18761 = intel, little-endian, 19789 = motorola, big-endian
                }
                file: switch byte_order {
                  'II' => Header {
                    version: u16le enum Version {42 = tiff, 43 = bigtiff}
                    ifd_offset: u32le
                    ifd: at(ifd_offset from window) tiff.Ifd.le
                    body: bytes[remaining]  aside
                    // The IFDs after IFD 0, in the order they are linked. Each IFD starts at
                        the offset in the next_ifd_offset field of the IFD before it, counted
                        from the start of the TIFF. In an EXIF block, IFD 1 has the thumbnail.
                        In a multi-page TIFF, each page has its own IFD.
                    next_ifds: chain(first ifd.next_ifd_offset == ifd_offset ? 0 :
                        ifd.next_ifd_offset, next .next_ifd_offset, from window) tiff.Ifd.le
                        element name from words("ifd {}", index + 1)
                  }
                  'MM' => Header {
                    version: u16be enum Version {42 = tiff, 43 = bigtiff}
                    ifd_offset: u32be
                    ifd: at(ifd_offset from window) tiff.Ifd.be
                    body: bytes[remaining]  aside
                    // The IFDs after IFD 0, in the order they are linked. Each IFD starts at
                        the offset in the next_ifd_offset field of the IFD before it, counted
                        from the start of the TIFF. In an EXIF block, IFD 1 has the thumbnail.
                        In a multi-page TIFF, each page has its own IFD.
                    next_ifds: chain(first ifd.next_ifd_offset == ifd_offset ? 0 :
                        ifd.next_ifd_offset, next .next_ifd_offset, from window) tiff.Ifd.be
                        element name from words("ifd {}", index + 1)
                  }
                  _ => bytes[remaining]
                }
              }
            }
            "http://ns.adobe.com/xap/1.0/" => text[remaining] utf8
            "http://ns.adobe.com/xmp/extension/" => ExtendedXmp {
              // The MD5 of the whole extended XMP, as 32 hex digits. The main XMP packet names
                  it in xmpNote:HasExtendedXMP.
              guid: text[32] ascii
              // Length in bytes of the whole extended XMP, across all its segments.
              full_length: u32be
              // Where this piece starts within the whole extended XMP.
              offset: u32be
              text: text[remaining] utf8
            }
            _ => bytes[remaining]
          }
        }
      }
      0xfffe => Body (contents contents) {
        length: u16be
        contents: sized(length - 2) text[remaining] latin1
      }
      0xffda => Scan (line header then entropy) {
        length: u16be
        header: sized(length - 2) ScanHeader (line components then spectral_start worded "Ss {}"
            except "0" then spectral_end worded "Se {}" except "63" then approximation_high
            worded "Ah {}" except "0" then approximation_low worded "Al {}" except "0") {
          component_count: u8be
          components: ScanComponent (named by id, unit component, line dc_table worded "dc table
              {}" then ac_table worded "ac table {}") {
            id: u8be
            dc_table: u4be
            ac_table: u4be
          }[component_count]
          spectral_start: u8be
          spectral_end: u8be
          approximation_high: u4be
          approximation_low: u4be
        }
        entropy: sized(tomarker(FF unless 00 D0 D1 D2 D3 D4 D5 D6 D7))
            decoded(jpeg baseline segments 2) repeat(until end) (i16le[8])[8]
      }
      _ => Body (contents contents) {
        length: u16be
        contents: sized(length - 2) bytes[remaining]
      }
    }
  }
  trailer: bytes[remaining]
  icc_profile: stitched(from segments[].body.contents.data.piece) optional(when 0 < remaining)
      sized(remaining) ICCProfile {
    header: Header {
      // The size of the whole profile in bytes, header, tag table and tag data included.
      profile_size: u32be
      // The preferred colour management module (CMM), as a registered signature. Four zero
          bytes when no CMM is named.
      preferred_cmm: text[4] padded with 0x00 latin1
      version: Version (inline, line major worded "major" then minor worded "minor" then bugfix
          worded "bug fix") {
        major: u8be
        minor: u4be
        bugfix: u4be
        reserved: u16be
      }
      device_class: u32be enum DeviceClass {
        0x73636e72 = scnr, input device, 0x6d6e7472 = mntr, display device,
        0x70727472 = prtr, output device, 0x6c696e6b = link, device link,
        0x73706163 = spac, colour space, 0x61627374 = abst, abstract,
        0x6e6d636c = nmcl, named colour
      }
      // The data colour space, such as RGB or CMYK. In a device link, the first device's colour
          space.
      colour_space: u32be enum ColourSpace {
        0x58595a20 = XYZ, 0x4c616220 = Lab, 0x4c757620 = Luv, 0x59436272 = YCbCr,
        0x59787920 = Yxy, 0x52474220 = RGB, 0x47524159 = grey, 0x48535620 = HSV,
        0x484c5320 = HLS, 0x434d594b = CMYK, 0x434d5920 = CMY, 0x32434c52 = 2 colours,
        0x33434c52 = 3 colours, 0x34434c52 = 4 colours, 0x35434c52 = 5 colours,
        0x36434c52 = 6 colours, 0x37434c52 = 7 colours, 0x38434c52 = 8 colours,
        0x39434c52 = 9 colours, 0x41434c52 = 10 colours, 0x42434c52 = 11 colours,
        0x43434c52 = 12 colours, 0x44434c52 = 13 colours, 0x45434c52 = 14 colours,
        0x46434c52 = 15 colours
      }
      // The profile connection space, XYZ or Lab. In a device link, the second device's colour
          space.
      pcs: u32be enum ColourSpace {
        0x58595a20 = XYZ, 0x4c616220 = Lab, 0x4c757620 = Luv, 0x59436272 = YCbCr,
        0x59787920 = Yxy, 0x52474220 = RGB, 0x47524159 = grey, 0x48535620 = HSV,
        0x484c5320 = HLS, 0x434d594b = CMYK, 0x434d5920 = CMY, 0x32434c52 = 2 colours,
        0x33434c52 = 3 colours, 0x34434c52 = 4 colours, 0x35434c52 = 5 colours,
        0x36434c52 = 6 colours, 0x37434c52 = 7 colours, 0x38434c52 = 8 colours,
        0x39434c52 = 9 colours, 0x41434c52 = 10 colours, 0x42434c52 = 11 colours,
        0x43434c52 = 12 colours, 0x44434c52 = 13 colours, 0x45434c52 = 14 colours,
        0x46434c52 = 15 colours
      }
      // When the profile was first created, in UTC.
      created: dateTimeNumber {
        year: u16be
        month: u16be
        day: u16be
        hour: u16be
        minute: u16be
        second: u16be
      }
      signature: magic "acsp"
      platform: u32be enum Platform {
        0x00 = none, 0x4150504c = Apple, 0x4d534654 = Microsoft, 0x53474920 = Silicon Graphics,
        0x53554e57 = Sun Microsystems, 0x54474e54 = Taligent
      }
      // Bit 0 is set when the profile is embedded in a file. Bit 1 is set when it must only be
          used with the colour data it is embedded with.
      flags: u32be flags ProfileFlags {bit 0 = embedded, bit 1 = use with embedded data only}
      manufacturer: text[4] padded with 0x00 latin1
      model: text[4] padded with 0x00 latin1
      // Bits 0 to 3 describe the media, clear or set: bit 0 reflective or transparency, bit 1
          glossy or matte, bit 2 positive or negative, bit 3 colour or black and white.
      attributes: u64be flags DeviceAttributes {
        bit 0 = transparency, bit 1 = matte, bit 2 = negative, bit 3 = black and white
      }
      rendering_intent: u32be enum RenderingIntent {
        0 = perceptual, 1 = media-relative colorimetric, 2 = saturation,
        3 = ICC-absolute colorimetric
      }
      // The PCS illuminant, which the spec fixes at D50: X 0.9642, Y 1.0, Z 0.8249.
      illuminant: XYZNumber (inline, line x worded "X" then y worded "Y" then z worded "Z") {
        x: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
          stored: i32be
          worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
        }
        y: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
          stored: i32be
          worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
        }
        z: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
          stored: i32be
          worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
        }
      }
      creator: text[4] padded with 0x00 latin1
      // MD5 hash of the whole profile, calculated with the flags, rendering intent and profile
          ID fields taken as zero. All 16 bytes are zero when no ID was calculated.
      profile_id: bytes[16]
      // 28 bytes reserved by the ICC, which must be zero.
      reserved: bytes[28]
    }
    tag_count: u32be
    tags: TagEntry (named by tag, contents data, unit tag) {
      tag: u32be enum TagSignature {
        0x41324230 = A2B0, device to PCS, perceptual,
        0x41324231 = A2B1, device to PCS, colorimetric,
        0x41324232 = A2B2, device to PCS, saturation,
        0x42324130 = B2A0, PCS to device, perceptual,
        0x42324131 = B2A1, PCS to device, colorimetric,
        0x42324132 = B2A2, PCS to device, saturation,
        0x42324430 = B2D0, PCS to device, perceptual, floating point,
        0x42324431 = B2D1, PCS to device, media-relative, floating point,
        0x42324432 = B2D2, PCS to device, saturation, floating point,
        0x42324433 = B2D3, PCS to device, ICC-absolute, floating point,
        0x44324230 = D2B0, device to PCS, perceptual, floating point,
        0x44324231 = D2B1, device to PCS, media-relative, floating point,
        0x44324232 = D2B2, device to PCS, saturation, floating point,
        0x44324233 = D2B3, device to PCS, ICC-absolute, floating point,
        0x7258595a = rXYZ, red matrix column, 0x6758595a = gXYZ, green matrix column,
        0x6258595a = bXYZ, blue matrix column, 0x72545243 = rTRC, red tone reproduction curve,
        0x67545243 = gTRC, green tone reproduction curve,
        0x62545243 = bTRC, blue tone reproduction curve,
        0x6b545243 = kTRC, grey tone reproduction curve, 0x77747074 = wtpt, media white point,
        0x626b7074 = bkpt, media black point, 0x63616c74 = calt, calibration date and time,
        0x74617267 = targ, characterisation target, 0x63686164 = chad, chromatic adaptation,
        0x6368726d = chrm, chromaticity, 0x63696370 = cicp, coding-independent code points,
        0x636c726f = clro, colorant order, 0x636c7274 = clrt, colorant table,
        0x636c6f74 = clot, colorant table out,
        0x63696973 = ciis, colorimetric intent image state, 0x63707274 = cprt, copyright,
        0x64657363 = desc, profile description,
        0x646d6e64 = dmnd, device manufacturer description,
        0x646d6464 = dmdd, device model description, 0x67616d74 = gamt, gamut,
        0x6c756d69 = lumi, luminance, 0x6d656173 = meas, measurement,
        0x6d657461 = meta, metadata, 0x6e636f6c = ncol, named colour,
        0x6e636c32 = ncl2, named colour 2, 0x72657370 = resp, output response,
        0x72696730 = rig0, perceptual rendering intent gamut,
        0x72696732 = rig2, saturation rendering intent gamut, 0x70726530 = pre0, preview 0,
        0x70726531 = pre1, preview 1, 0x70726532 = pre2, preview 2,
        0x70736571 = pseq, profile sequence description,
        0x70736964 = psid, profile sequence identifier, 0x74656368 = tech, technology,
        0x76756564 = vued, viewing conditions description,
        0x76696577 = view, viewing conditions, 0x70736430 = psd0, PostScript CRD 0,
        0x70736431 = psd1, PostScript CRD 1, 0x70736432 = psd2, PostScript CRD 2,
        0x70736433 = psd3, PostScript CRD 3, 0x70733273 = ps2s, PostScript colour space array,
        0x70733269 = ps2i, PostScript rendering intent,
        0x73637264 = scrd, screening description, 0x7363726e = scrn, screening,
        0x62666420 = bfd, undercolour removal and black generation,
        0x64657673 = devs, device settings, 0x63726469 = crdi, CRD info,
        0x76636774 = vcgt, video card gamma (Apple private tag),
        0x6473636d = dscm, localised description (Apple private tag)
      }
      // Where the tag's data starts, counted from the first byte of the profile.
      offset: u32be
      // Size of the tag's data in bytes. The spec leaves out the padding after the data, but
          some writers count it.
      size: u32be
      data: at(offset from window) sized(size) TagData (contents contents) {
        type: u32be enum TagType {
          0x6368726d = chrm, chromaticity, 0x63696370 = cicp, coding-independent code points,
          0x636c726f = clro, colorant order, 0x636c7274 = clrt, colorant table,
          0x63757276 = curv, curve, 0x64617461 = data, ASCII or binary data,
          0x6474696d = dtim, date and time, 0x64696374 = dict, dictionary,
          0x6d667432 = mft2, 16-bit lookup table, 0x6d667431 = mft1, 8-bit lookup table,
          0x6d414220 = mAB, A to B lookup, 0x6d424120 = mBA, B to A lookup,
          0x6d656173 = meas, measurement, 0x6d6c7563 = mluc, multi-localized Unicode,
          0x6d706574 = mpet, multi-process elements, 0x6e636c32 = ncl2, named colour 2,
          0x70617261 = para, parametric curve, 0x70736571 = pseq, profile sequence description,
          0x70736964 = psid, profile sequence identifier,
          0x72637332 = rcs2, response curve set, 0x73663332 = sf32, s15Fixed16 array,
          0x73696720 = sig, signature, 0x74657874 = text, ASCII text,
          0x75663332 = uf32, u16Fixed16 array, 0x75693038 = ui08, 8-bit integer array,
          0x75693136 = ui16, 16-bit integer array, 0x75693332 = ui32, 32-bit integer array,
          0x75693634 = ui64, 64-bit integer array, 0x76696577 = view, viewing conditions,
          0x58595a20 = XYZ, XYZ values, 0x64657363 = desc, text description,
          0x6e636f6c = ncol, named colour, 0x7363726e = scrn, screening,
          0x62666420 = bfd, undercolour removal and black generation,
          0x63726469 = crdi, CRD info, 0x64657673 = devs, device settings,
          0x76636774 = vcgt, video card gamma (Apple private type)
        }
        reserved: u32be
        contents: switch type {
          'desc' => textDescriptionType {
            // Bytes of ASCII text, its nul included.
            ascii_count: u32be
            ascii: text[ascii_count] padded with 0x00 ascii
            unicode_language: switch 1 * (3 < remaining) {1 => u32be, _ => bytes[0]}
            // UTF-16 code units of text, two bytes each, its nul included.
            unicode_count: switch 1 * (3 < remaining) {1 => u32be, _ => bytes[0]}
            unicode: switch 1 * (0 < remaining) {
              1 => text[unicode_count * 2] padded with 0x00 utf16be
              _ => bytes[0]
            }
            scriptcode_code: switch 1 * (1 < remaining) {1 => u16be, _ => bytes[0]}
            scriptcode_count: switch 1 * (0 < remaining) {1 => u8be, _ => bytes[0]}
            // 67 bytes, of which the first scriptcode_count are the Macintosh description.
            scriptcode: switch 1 * (66 < remaining) {
              1 => text[67] padded with 0x00 latin1
              _ => bytes[0]
            }
          }
          'mluc' => multiLocalizedUnicodeType {
            record_count: u32be
            // Bytes per record: 12.
            record_size: u32be
            records: LocalizedString (contents string, unit string) {
              language: text[2] padded with 0x00 ascii
              country: text[2] padded with 0x00 ascii
              length: u32be
              offset: u32be
              string: at(offset from window) text[length] utf16be
            }[record_count]
          }
          'text' => textType (contents text) {
            text: text[remaining] padded with 0x00 ascii
          }
          'XYZ ' => XYZType (contents xyz) {
            xyz: switch remaining / 12 {
              1 => XYZNumber (inline, line x worded "X" then y worded "Y" then z worded "Z") {
                x: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                  stored: i32be
                  worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                }
                y: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                  stored: i32be
                  worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                }
                z: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                  stored: i32be
                  worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                }
              }
              _ => XYZNumber (inline, line x worded "X" then y worded "Y" then z worded "Z") {
                x: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                  stored: i32be
                  worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                }
                y: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                  stored: i32be
                  worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                }
                z: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                  stored: i32be
                  worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                }
              }[remaining / 12]
            }
          }
          'curv' => curveType {
            // Number of entries. 0 means the identity curve, and with 1 the entry is the gamma
                exponent.
            count: u32be
            // The exponent: Y = X^gamma.
            gamma: optional(when count == 1) u8Fixed8Number (inline, payload stored, line worth)
                {
              stored: u16be
              worth: computed real stored / 256.0
            }
            // Y values of the curve at evenly spaced X from 0.0 to 1.0. Each Y value is stored
                from 0 for 0.0 to 65535 for 1.0.
            entries: optional(when count > 1) u16be[count]
          }
          'para' => parametricCurveType {
            function: u16be enum ParametricFunction {
              0 = Y = X^g, 1 = Y = (aX + b)^g for X ≥ -b/a, else 0 (CIE 122-1966),
              2 = Y = (aX + b)^g + c for X ≥ -b/a, else c (IEC 61966-3),
              3 = Y = (aX + b)^g for X ≥ d, else cX (IEC 61966-2-1, sRGB),
              4 = Y = (aX + b)^g + e for X ≥ d, else cX + f
            }
            reserved: u16be
            g: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: i32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }
            a: optional(when function >= 1 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: i32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }
            b: optional(when function >= 1 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: i32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }
            c: optional(when function >= 2 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: i32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }
            d: optional(when function >= 3 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: i32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }
            e: optional(when function >= 4 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: i32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }
            f: optional(when function >= 4 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: i32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }
          }
          'sf32' => s15Fixed16ArrayType (contents values) {
            values: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: i32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }[remaining / 4]
          }
          'uf32' => u16Fixed16ArrayType (contents values) {
            values: u16Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: u32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }[remaining / 4]
          }
          'ui08' => uInt8ArrayType (contents values) {
            values: u8be[remaining / 1]
          }
          'ui16' => uInt16ArrayType (contents values) {
            values: u16be[remaining / 2]
          }
          'ui32' => uInt32ArrayType (contents values) {
            values: u32be[remaining / 4]
          }
          'ui64' => uInt64ArrayType (contents values) {
            values: u64be[remaining / 8]
          }
          'sig ' => signatureType (contents signature) {
            signature: switch tag {
              'tech' => u32be enum Technology {
                0x6673636e = fscn, film scanner, 0x6463616d = dcam, digital camera,
                0x7273636e = rscn, reflective scanner, 0x696a6574 = ijet, ink jet printer,
                0x74776178 = twax, thermal wax printer,
                0x6570686f = epho, electrophotographic printer,
                0x65737461 = esta, electrostatic printer,
                0x64737562 = dsub, dye sublimation printer,
                0x7270686f = rpho, photographic paper printer, 0x6670726e = fprn, film writer,
                0x7669646d = vidm, video monitor, 0x76696463 = vidc, video camera,
                0x706a7476 = pjtv, projection television,
                0x43525420 = CRT, cathode ray tube display,
                0x504d4420 = PMD, passive matrix display,
                0x414d4420 = AMD, active matrix display,
                0x4c434420 = LCD, liquid crystal display,
                0x4f4c4544 = OLED, organic LED display, 0x4b504344 = KPCD, Photo CD,
                0x696d6773 = imgs, photographic image setter, 0x67726176 = grav, gravure,
                0x6f666673 = offs, offset lithography, 0x73696c6b = silk, silkscreen,
                0x666c6578 = flex, flexography, 0x6d706673 = mpfs, motion picture film scanner,
                0x6d706672 = mpfr, motion picture film recorder,
                0x646d7063 = dmpc, digital motion picture camera,
                0x6463706a = dcpj, digital cinema projector
              }
              'ciis' => u32be enum ImageState {
                0x73636f65 = scoe, scene colorimetry estimates,
                0x73617065 = sape, scene appearance estimates,
                0x66706365 = fpce, focal plane colorimetry estimates,
                0x72686f63 = rhoc, reflection hardcopy original colorimetry,
                0x72706f63 = rpoc, reflection print output colorimetry
              }
              _ => text[4] padded with 0x00 latin1
            }
          }
          'meas' => measurementType {
            observer: u32be enum StandardObserver {
              0 = unknown, 1 = CIE 1931 standard observer, 2 = CIE 1964 standard observer
            }
            // The nCIEXYZ tristimulus values of the measurement backing.
            backing: XYZNumber (inline, line x worded "X" then y worded "Y" then z worded "Z") {
              x: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              y: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              z: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
            }
            geometry: u32be enum MeasurementGeometry {
              0 = unknown, 1 = 0°:45° or 45°:0°, 2 = 0°:d or d:0°
            }
            // Measurement flare, from 0.0 for 0% to 1.0 for 100%.
            flare: u16Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
              stored: u32be
              worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
            }
            illuminant: u32be enum StandardIlluminant {
              0 = unknown, 1 = D50, 2 = D65, 3 = D93, 4 = F2, 5 = D55, 6 = A,
              7 = E, equi-power, 8 = F8
            }
          }
          'view' => viewingConditionsType {
            // Un-normalized CIEXYZ of the illuminant, with Y in cd/m².
            illuminant: XYZNumber (inline, line x worded "X" then y worded "Y" then z worded
                "Z") {
              x: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              y: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              z: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
            }
            // Un-normalized CIEXYZ of the surround, with Y in cd/m².
            surround: XYZNumber (inline, line x worded "X" then y worded "Y" then z worded "Z")
                {
              x: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              y: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              z: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
            }
            illuminant_type: u32be enum StandardIlluminant {
              0 = unknown, 1 = D50, 2 = D65, 3 = D93, 4 = F2, 5 = D55, 6 = A,
              7 = E, equi-power, 8 = F8
            }
          }
          'chrm' => chromaticityType {
            channel_count: u16be
            colorant: u16be enum Colorant {
              0 = unknown, 1 = ITU-R BT.709-2, 2 = SMPTE RP145, 3 = EBU Tech. 3213-E, 4 = P22,
              5 = P3, 6 = ITU-R BT.2020
            }
            channels: xy (inline, line x worded "x" then y worded "y") {
              x: u16Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: u32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              y: u16Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: u32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
            }[channel_count]
          }
          'dtim' => dateTimeType {
            date_time: dateTimeNumber {
              year: u16be
              month: u16be
              day: u16be
              hour: u16be
              minute: u16be
              second: u16be
            }
          }
          'data' => dataType {
            flag: u32be enum DataFlag {0 = ASCII, 1 = binary}
            data: switch flag {
              0 => text[remaining] padded with 0x00 ascii
              _ => bytes[remaining]
            }
          }
          'cicp' => cicpType {
            colour_primaries: u8be enum ColourPrimaries {
              1 = BT.709, sRGB, 2 = unspecified, 4 = BT.470 System M,
              5 = BT.470 System B, G (BT.601 625-line), 6 = SMPTE 170M (BT.601 525-line),
              7 = SMPTE 240M, 8 = generic film (illuminant C), 9 = BT.2020, BT.2100,
              10 = SMPTE ST 428-1 (CIE 1931 XYZ), 11 = SMPTE ST 431-2 (DCI-P3),
              12 = SMPTE ST 432-1 (Display P3), 22 = EBU Tech. 3213-E
            }
            transfer_characteristics: u8be enum TransferCharacteristics {
              1 = BT.709, 2 = unspecified, 4 = gamma 2.2 (BT.470 System M),
              5 = gamma 2.8 (BT.470 System B, G), 6 = SMPTE 170M (BT.601), 7 = SMPTE 240M,
              8 = linear, 9 = logarithmic, 100:1 range, 10 = logarithmic, 100√10:1 range,
              11 = IEC 61966-2-4 (xvYCC), 12 = BT.1361 extended colour gamut,
              13 = IEC 61966-2-1 (sRGB), 14 = BT.2020, 10-bit, 15 = BT.2020, 12-bit,
              16 = SMPTE ST 2084 (PQ), 17 = SMPTE ST 428-1, 18 = ARIB STD-B67 (HLG)
            }
            matrix_coefficients: u8be enum MatrixCoefficients {
              0 = identity (RGB), 1 = BT.709, 2 = unspecified, 4 = FCC 73.682,
              5 = BT.470 System B, G (BT.601 625-line), 6 = SMPTE 170M (BT.601 525-line),
              7 = SMPTE 240M, 8 = YCgCo, 9 = BT.2020 non-constant luminance,
              10 = BT.2020 constant luminance, 11 = SMPTE ST 2085 (Y'D'zD'x),
              12 = chromaticity-derived non-constant luminance,
              13 = chromaticity-derived constant luminance, 14 = ICtCp (BT.2100),
              15 = IPT-C2 (SMPTE ST 2128), 16 = YCgCo-R, even, 17 = YCgCo-R, odd
            }
            video_full_range_flag: u8be enum VideoFullRange {0 = narrow range, 1 = full range}
          }
          'clro' => colorantOrderType {
            count: u32be
            order: u8be[count]
          }
          'clrt' => colorantTableType {
            count: u32be
            colorants: Colorant (unit colorant) {
              name: text[32] padded with 0x00 ascii
              pcs: u16be[3]
            }[count]
          }
          'ncl2' => namedColor2Type {
            vendor_flags: u32be
            count: u32be
            device_coordinates: u32be
            prefix: text[32] padded with 0x00 ascii
            suffix: text[32] padded with 0x00 ascii
            colours: NamedColour (unit colour) {
              name: text[32] padded with 0x00 ascii
              pcs: u16be[3]
              device: u16be[device_coordinates]
            }[count]
          }
          'mft2' => lut16Type {
            input_channels: u8be
            output_channels: u8be
            grid_points: u8be
            padding: u8be
            // A 3 by 3 matrix applied before the input tables. It must be the identity unless
                the input colour space is XYZ.
            matrix: Matrix {
              e1: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e2: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e3: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e4: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e5: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e6: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e7: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e8: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e9: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
            }
            input_entries: u16be
            output_entries: u16be
            input_tables: u16be[input_entries * input_channels]
            clut: u16be[remaining / 2 - output_entries * output_channels]
            output_tables: u16be[output_entries * output_channels]
          }
          'mft1' => lut8Type {
            input_channels: u8be
            output_channels: u8be
            grid_points: u8be
            padding: u8be
            // A 3 by 3 matrix applied before the input tables. It must be the identity unless
                the input colour space is XYZ.
            matrix: Matrix {
              e1: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e2: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e3: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e4: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e5: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e6: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e7: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e8: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e9: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
            }
            input_tables: u8be[256 * input_channels]
            clut: u8be[remaining - 256 * output_channels]
            output_tables: u8be[256 * output_channels]
          }
          'mAB ' => lutAToBType {
            input_channels: u8be
            output_channels: u8be
            padding: u16be
            b_curves_offset: u32be
            matrix_offset: u32be
            m_curves_offset: u32be
            clut_offset: u32be
            a_curves_offset: u32be
            b_curves: optional(when b_curves_offset != 0) at(b_curves_offset from window) Curve
                (contents contents, unit curve) {
              type: u32be enum TagType {
                0x6368726d = chrm, chromaticity,
                0x63696370 = cicp, coding-independent code points,
                0x636c726f = clro, colorant order, 0x636c7274 = clrt, colorant table,
                0x63757276 = curv, curve, 0x64617461 = data, ASCII or binary data,
                0x6474696d = dtim, date and time, 0x64696374 = dict, dictionary,
                0x6d667432 = mft2, 16-bit lookup table, 0x6d667431 = mft1, 8-bit lookup table,
                0x6d414220 = mAB, A to B lookup, 0x6d424120 = mBA, B to A lookup,
                0x6d656173 = meas, measurement, 0x6d6c7563 = mluc, multi-localized Unicode,
                0x6d706574 = mpet, multi-process elements, 0x6e636c32 = ncl2, named colour 2,
                0x70617261 = para, parametric curve,
                0x70736571 = pseq, profile sequence description,
                0x70736964 = psid, profile sequence identifier,
                0x72637332 = rcs2, response curve set, 0x73663332 = sf32, s15Fixed16 array,
                0x73696720 = sig, signature, 0x74657874 = text, ASCII text,
                0x75663332 = uf32, u16Fixed16 array, 0x75693038 = ui08, 8-bit integer array,
                0x75693136 = ui16, 16-bit integer array,
                0x75693332 = ui32, 32-bit integer array,
                0x75693634 = ui64, 64-bit integer array, 0x76696577 = view, viewing conditions,
                0x58595a20 = XYZ, XYZ values, 0x64657363 = desc, text description,
                0x6e636f6c = ncol, named colour, 0x7363726e = scrn, screening,
                0x62666420 = bfd, undercolour removal and black generation,
                0x63726469 = crdi, CRD info, 0x64657673 = devs, device settings,
                0x76636774 = vcgt, video card gamma (Apple private type)
              }
              reserved: u32be
              contents: switch type {
                'curv' => curveType {
                  // Number of entries. 0 means the identity curve, and with 1 the entry is the
                      gamma exponent.
                  count: u32be
                  // The exponent: Y = X^gamma.
                  gamma: optional(when count == 1) u8Fixed8Number
                      (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                    stored: u16be
                    worth: computed real stored / 256.0
                  }
                  // Y values of the curve at evenly spaced X from 0.0 to 1.0. Each Y value is
                      stored from 0 for 0.0 to 65535 for 1.0.
                  entries: optional(when count > 1) u16be[count]
                }
                'para' => parametricCurveType {
                  function: u16be enum ParametricFunction {
                    0 = Y = X^g, 1 = Y = (aX + b)^g for X ≥ -b/a, else 0 (CIE 122-1966),
                    2 = Y = (aX + b)^g + c for X ≥ -b/a, else c (IEC 61966-3),
                    3 = Y = (aX + b)^g for X ≥ d, else cX (IEC 61966-2-1, sRGB),
                    4 = Y = (aX + b)^g + e for X ≥ d, else cX + f
                  }
                  reserved: u16be
                  g: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                    stored: i32be
                    worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                  }
                  a: optional(when function >= 1 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                      (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                    stored: i32be
                    worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                  }
                  b: optional(when function >= 1 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                      (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                    stored: i32be
                    worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                  }
                  c: optional(when function >= 2 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                      (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                    stored: i32be
                    worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                  }
                  d: optional(when function >= 3 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                      (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                    stored: i32be
                    worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                  }
                  e: optional(when function >= 4 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                      (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                    stored: i32be
                    worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                  }
                  f: optional(when function >= 4 and function <= 4) s15Fixed16Number
                      (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                    stored: i32be
                    worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
                  }
                }
                _ => bytes[0]
              }
              padding: u(min(padding(sizeof(type) + sizeof(reserved) + sizeof(contents), 4),
                  remaining) * 8)be
            }[output_channels]
            // A 3 by 3 matrix, e1 to e9 by rows, then the offsets e10 to e12.
            matrix: optional(when matrix_offset != 0) at(matrix_offset from window) Matrix {
              e1: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e2: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e3: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e4: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e5: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e6: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e7: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e8: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e9: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e10: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e11: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
              e12: s15Fixed16Number (inline, payload stored, line worth) {
                stored: i32be
                worth: computed real stored / 65536.0
              }
            }
            m_curves: optional(when m_curves_offset != 0) at(m_curves_offset from window) Curve

                0x70617261 = para, parametric curve,
                0x70736571 = pseq, profile sequence description,
                0x70736964 = psid, profile sequence identifier,
                0x72637332 = rcs2, response curve set, 0x73663332 = sf32, s15Fixed16 array,

                0x75693332 = ui32, 32-bit integer array,
                0x75693634 = ui64, 64-bit integer array, 0x76696577 = view, viewing conditions,
                0x58595a20 = XYZ, XYZ values, 0x64657363 = desc, text description,

                  remaining) * 8)be

              // Number of grid points along each input dimension. Only the first input_channels
                  entries are used.

              // Bytes per value: 1 or 2.

              values: switch precision {
                1 => u8be[1 * (grid_points[0] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[0]) * (grid_points[1] == 0
                    ? 1 : grid_points[1]) * (grid_points[2] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[2]) *
                    (grid_points[3] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[3]) *
                    (grid_points[4] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[4]) *
                    (grid_points[5] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[5]) *
                    (grid_points[6] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[6]) *
                    (grid_points[7] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[7]) *
                    (grid_points[8] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[8]) *
                    (grid_points[9] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[9]) *
                    (grid_points[10] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[10]) *
                    (grid_points[11] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[11]) *
                    (grid_points[12] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[12]) *
                    (grid_points[13] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[13]) *
                    (grid_points[14] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[14]) *
                    (grid_points[15] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[15]) * output_channels]
                _ => u16be[1 * (grid_points[0] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[0]) * (grid_points[1] == 0
                    ? 1 : grid_points[1]) * (grid_points[2] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[2]) *
                    (grid_points[3] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[3]) *
                    (grid_points[4] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[4]) *
                    (grid_points[5] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[5]) *
                    (grid_points[6] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[6]) *
                    (grid_points[7] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[7]) *
                    (grid_points[8] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[8]) *
                    (grid_points[9] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[9]) *
                    (grid_points[10] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[10]) *
                    (grid_points[11] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[11]) *
                    (grid_points[12] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[12]) *
                    (grid_points[13] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[13]) *
                    (grid_points[14] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[14]) *
                    (grid_points[15] == 0 ? 1 : grid_points[15]) * output_channels]
              }
            }
